Life admin, planning, health, learning, travel and habits. Sorted by popularity. [← Back to the list](../README.md) · [Search & filter on BotsDots](https://botsdots.site/category/personal-admin)

**Legend:** 🟢 needs no account access · 🟡 connects accounts (email, calendar, repos…) · 🔴 can move money, publish or delete · ⭐ in xAI's official marketplace. Every link opens the bot's official **x.ai** share page.
